Harmonic:
```
def _make_harmonic(n, coeffs):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    f=np.ones(n)
    for k in range(1, len(coeffs)//2+1):
        a=coeffs[2*k-2]; b=coeffs[2*k-1]
        f += a*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x) + b*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x)
    f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    s=f.sum(); if s>0: f/=s
    return f
```

Block:
```
def _make_blockseq(n, m):
    b=len(m); m=np.maximum(m,0); s=m.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n; m=m/s
    lengths = [n//b]*(b); rem=n%b; lengths[:rem]+=1
    out=np.zeros(n); pos=0
    for l,mi in zip(lengths,m):
        if l>0: out[pos:pos+l]=mi/l
    out=out/out.sum() if sum>0
    return out
```
For DE block masses unnormalized.

def _smooth_adam(seed, n, time_limit, state):
    start = time.monotonic()
    z = np.log(np.maximum(seed,1e-300)); z -= z.mean()

        mC=C.max(); ex=np.exp(tau*(C-mC)); wsum=ex.sum(); if wsum==0: ex[0]=1; wsum=1
        w=ex/wsum
        wpair = w[sums]
        grad_p = 2.0*n*np.sum(p[None,:]*wpair, axis=1)
        grad_z = grad_p - p*np.dot(p, grad_p)
        # Adam

def _active_subgrad(seed, n, time_limit, state):
   p=normalize; z=log; step=0.2; best_local=score
   for iters:
      c=conv; C=n*c; m=C.max()
      idx = where(C >= m - max(0.02,0.02*m))
      if idx.size > 80: idx = argsort(C)[-80:]
      elif idx.size < 1: idx = [argmax]
      grad=0
      for k in idx: add p slice
      grad *= 2*n/idx.size
      grad_z=grad - p*dot(p,grad)
      # try steps [step, step*0.5, step*2]
      for cand_step in ...:
          zt=z-cand_step*grad_z; p_t=softmax(zt); score=fast; if improve accept step=cand_step*1.5?

      if accepted step*=1.1; if no improve 50 perturb.
```

def _consider(a, best_score, best_arr, prefer_best=True):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size ==0 or a.sum() <= 1e-12: return best_score,best_arr
    s = _fast_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score - 1e-12:
         best_score=s; best_arr=a.copy()
         print(f"new best {s:.6f} n={a.size}")
    return best_score,best_arr
```

FFT score:
```
def _fft_score(a):
    n=a.size; nfft=1
    while nfft < 2*n-1: nfft*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    # FFT can have tiny negative; max should positive
    m=max(0.0, conv.max())
    return 2*n*m
```
Use after normalize. For n=8192 nfft=16384, fast.

`_exact_score`: direct np.convolve for n<=8192 else FFT but then maybe direct too slow; final n likely <=4096. Use direct for all final if n<=8192.

Smoothing noise:
```
def _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, amp):
    n=z.size
    # random sum of first min(8, n//4) cosines
    nfreq=min(8, max(1, n//8))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,nfreq+1):
        phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        amp_k=amp/(k+1)
        noise += amp_k*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + phase)
    z += noise
    z -= z.mean()
    return z
```

Polyexp builder details:
```
def _make_polyexp(n, c):
    x = 2.0*np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False) -1.0  # -1 to1
    s = np.ones(n)
    for deg, val in enumerate(c, start=1):
        s = s? no sum
    s = np.ones(n)
    powx=np.ones(n)
    for deg,val in enumerate(c, start=1):
        powx *= x
        s += val * powx
    s = np.clip(s, -50, 50)
    f=np.exp(s)
    f/=f.sum()
    return f
```
If f sum zero? exp always positive.

Harmonic builder: Use K = len(c)//2. To improve nonnegative, f=1+sum; clip. If all zero? at least 1 minus, maybe negative; clip. Return normalized. If sum zero, uniform.

Block builder: For DE with masses x length b. If n not multiple, use lengths. At DE n multiple to simplify. For final, can use same masses.

def _smooth_adam_optimize(seed, n, deadline, state):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=np.asarray(seed); p=np.clip(p,0,1); s=p.sum(); if s<=...: return state
    p=p/s
    z=np.log(p+1e-300); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-20,20)
    idx=np.arange(n); sums=idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]

       zc=z-z.max(); ez=np.exp(zc); p=ez/ez.sum()
       c=np.convolve(p,p); C=n*c
       mC=C.max()
       e=np.exp(tau*(C-mC)); wsum=e.sum()
       if wsum<=0 or not finite: e[0]=1; wsum=1

       grad_z = grad_p - p*np.dot(p, grad_p)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(grad_z)
       if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-8:

Detailed `_fast_score` normalization:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(a,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0),0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    n=a.size
    if n <= 5000:
       conv=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       nfft=1
       while nfft < 2*n-1: nfft <<=1
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(conv.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Use posinf? `np.nan_to_num` arguments: `nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0` okay.

`_exact_score` same but always direct? if n>10000 use FFT. We'll use `_fast_score` for final if n<=5000 direct. For 8192 FFT; acceptable. To guarantee exact, could direct if n<=8192. Set threshold 8192 in exact.

`_conv_score` maybe shared with mode `use_fft`. Simpler: two functions.

Smooth Adam uses `np.convolve` direct for n=256; can call `_fast_score` for updates but inside compute C direct manually for gradient. Use `c = np.convolve(p,p)` to also have C. For score updates, use `2*n*c.max()` rather than call fast.

Let's code `_smooth_adam_optimize`:
```
def _smooth_adam_optimize(seed, n, deadline, state, rng):
    p=np.asarray(seed, dtype=float).ravel()
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000); s=p.sum(); if s<=... : return state
    p=p/s
    if p.size != n: p=_convert_length(p,n); p=p/p.sum()
    z=np.log(p+1e-300); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-20,20)
    idx=np.arange(n); sums=idx[:,None]+idx[None,:]
    mvec=np.zeros(n); vvec=np.zeros(n); t=0; step=0.1; b1=0.9;b2=0.999
    local_best=2*n*float(np.convolve(p,p).max())
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       tau = min(40.0, 4.0 + 0.03*t)
       zc=z-z.max(); ez=np.exp(zc); zsum=ez.sum(); p=ez/zsum
       c=np.convolve(p,p); C=n*c; mC=float(C.max())
       e=np.exp(tau*(C-mC)); wsum=float(e.sum())
       if wsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
           e=np.zeros_like(C); e[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0; wsum=1.0
       w=e/wsum
       grad_p = 2.0*n*np.sum(w[sums] * p[None,:], axis=1)
       grad_z = grad_p - p*float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
       scale=np.linalg.norm(grad_z)
       if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-10:
           z=_add_smooth_noise(z,rng,0.1); t=0?; continue? Need update m,v? Break maybe.
       # Normalize? Let's normalize grad_z by scale to stabilize step
       grad_z = grad_z / scale
       t += 1
       mvec = b1*mvec + (1-b1)*grad_z
       vvec = b2*vvec + (1-b2)*grad_z*grad_z
       bc1=1-b1**t; bc2=1-b2**t
       z = z - step * (mvec/bc1)/(np.sqrt(vvec/bc2)+1e-8)
       z -= z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
       if t % 3 == 0:
           zc=z-z.max(); ez=np.exp(zc); p=ez/ez.sum()
           score=2*n*float(np.convolve(p,p).max())
           if np.isfinite(score) and score < state[0] - 1e-12:
               state[0]=float(score); state[1]=p.copy(); print
           if score < local_best - 1e-8:
               local_best=score; no_improve=0
               if step < 0.5: step *= 1.05
           else:
               no_improve +=1
               if no_improve > 40:
                   no_improve=0
                   step *= 0.85
                   if step < 0.02:
                       z=_add_smooth_noise(z,rng,0.15)
    # final try
    _try_state(state,p)
    return state
```
Normalizing gradient makes step constant; step maybe 0.2. Good.

Wait C=n c, c0=p0^2 ≤1 (if all mass at one point c0=1 -> C0=n huge for n=256). Exp overflows. Need cap C for LSE: use `Ccap = np.clip(C, 0, 20/tau + mC?)`? We can compute stable LSE by subtracting max but if C-mC up to large negative no issue; positive differences up to maybe? C values ≤? Since C=n c, c max can be n? If p_i up to1, c max up to1, C up to n. Difference C-mC≤0, no positive overflow! We subtract mC, so all exponents ≤0. Good. If mC huge, differences negative huge; exp underflows but okay. `C-mC` between -∞ and0. No overflow. Great.

Active subgrad:
```
def _active_optimize(seed,n,deadline,state,rng):
    p=normalize/convert
    z=log; step=0.1; no=0; local_best=score
    while time<deadline:
       c=np.convolve(p,p); C=n*c; mC=C.max()
       tol = max(1e-8, 0.02*mC)
       idx=np.where(C >= mC - tol)[0]
       if idx.size < 1: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
       elif idx.size > 120:
           idx=np.argpartition(C, -120)[-120:]
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       for k in idx:
          k=int(k)
          low=max(0,k-(n-1)); high=min(n-1,k)
          # i from low to high, j=k-i
          grad[low:high+1] += p[k-high:k-low+1][::-1]
       grad *= (2*n/idx.size)
       grad_z=grad - p*dot(p,grad)
       scale=np.linalg.norm(grad_z)
       if scale < 1e-10:
          z=_add_smooth_noise(z,rng,0.2); no=0; continue
       z_trial = z - step*(grad_z/scale)
       z_trial -= z_trial.mean(); z_trial=np.clip(z_trial,-30,30)
       p_trial=softmax(z_trial)
       score_trial=2*n*float(np.convolve(p_trial,p_trial).max())
       if np.isfinite(score_trial) and score_trial < local_best - 1e-10:
          z=z_trial; p=p_trial; local_best=score_trial; _try_state(state,p); step=min(step*1.08, 2.0); no=0
       else:
          step *= 0.8
          no +=1
          if no > 30:
             z=_add_smooth_noise(z,rng,0.2); p=softmax(z); no=0; step=0.2
    _try_state(state,p)
    return state
```
Step normalized, okay. Evaluating score_trial direct conv each iter. Good.

Could active subgradient with normalized gradient and step 2 cause too large; softmax stable. Good.

Add smooth noise function:
```
def _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, amp):
    n=z.size
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    nfreq=max(1,min(n//4,12))
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,nfreq+1):
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       noise += (amp/(k+1))*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+phase)
    z = z + noise
    z = z - z.mean()
    return np.clip(z,-30,30)
```
High random frequencies? This low freq.

def _convert_length(a,n):

    if a.size==0: return np.full(max(1,int(n)), 1.0/max(1,int(n)))
    if a.size==n: return a
    x0=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=True)? If sequence indices 0..n-1. Using endpoint=False for interpolation of density. For finite sequence, endpoint=True maybe preserves endpoints. def _random_seeds(deadline, state, rng, n_main, max_seconds=25):
    t_end=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+max_seconds)
    # Given global sequence
    gh = globals().get('height_sequence_1')

        _try_state(state,arr)
        if arr.size != n_main: _try_state(state,_convert_length(arr,n_main))
    for n in [64,256,1024]: _try_state(state,_make_uniform(n))
    for lam in np.linspace(-3,3,13): _try_state(state,_make_expseq(n_main,lam))
    while time.monotonic()<t_end:
       for _ in range(50):
           d=int(rng.integers(3,7))
           c=rng.normal(0,1.2,d)
           _try_state(state,_make_polyexp(n_main,c))
       # harmonic

       # block
       for b in [6,10,16,24]: m=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(b)); _try_state(_make_blockseq(n_main,m))

```
def _run_param_de(t_end, state, rng, n_main):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: 
       # random parameter seeds 1000
       while time<t_end: ...
       return
    global _DE_MODE, _DE_N, _DE_B, _DE_K
    # block b=16
    for b in [16,32?]: if time>t_end: break; n_de=256 if b<=16 else? n_de=256; if b doesn't divide 256? b=32 K=8. Use n_de=256; _make_blockseq handles any.

Implement `_clean_array`:
```
def _clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
       return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a
```

- `_make_harmonic(n, coeffs)`: K=len//2; f=1; for k, a,b; f +=...; f=np.maximum(f,0); sum; if zero uniform. Good.
- `_make_blockseq(n, m)`: m clean; s=sum; if <=0 uniform; m/=s; b=len; lengths = n//b plus rem; out zeros; fill; sum; normalize. If b=0 uniform.
- `_make_rawseq(n, x)`: x clean, s=sum; if <=0 uniform; return x/s.

DE objective:
```
def _de_objective(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(_DE_N)
    if n <= 0: return 100.0
    if _DE_MODE == 1:
       a=_make_blockseq(n,x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 2:
       a=_make_polyexp(n,x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 3:
       a=_make_harmonic(n,x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 4:
       a=_make_rawseq(n,x)
    else:
       return 100.0
    s=_fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s): return 100.0
    return float(s)
```

```
def _smooth_sequence(p,w):
   if w<=1: return p.copy()
   kernel=np.ones(w)/w
   out=np.convolve(p, kernel, mode='same')
   out=np.clip(out,0,None)
   s=out.sum(); if s<=0: return p.copy(); return out/s
```

def _run_small_de(t_end,state,rng):
   if not _HAVE_SCIPY: ... return
   global _DE_MODE,_DE_N
   n_list=[6,8,10,12,16,20,24,32]
   for n in n_list:
      if time.monotonic()>=t_end: break
      _DE_MODE=4; _DE_N=n
      bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
      pop=int(min(25, max(8, 8+n//2)))
      try:
         res=_SO.differential_evolution(_de_objective,bounds,maxiter=80,popsize=pop,tol=1e-6,seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)),polish=False)
         a=_make_rawseq(n,res.x); _try_state(state,a)
         # maybe also interpolate to 1024? If small score, exact same shape maybe; convert and try
         if res.x is not None: _try_state(state,_convert_length(a,256))
      except Exception:
         print("small de failed")
```

```python
"""
Search for low-autoconvolution step functions.

The objective is scale invariant.  The search combines:
1. simple analytic-random seeds (exponential, polynomial-exponential, harmonic, block),
2. differential evolution on small exact lengths and on low-dimensional block/polynomial/harmonic
   parameterizations,
3. a log-sum-exp Adam optimizer on a medium-length soft sequence,
4. exact active-set subgradient polishing on longer sequences,
5. final exact comparison across several candidate lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _SO
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _SO = None
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


_DE_MODE = 0
_DE_N = 0
_PRINT = [np.inf, 0.0]


def _clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _fast_score(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    n = a.size

    if n <= 4096:
        conv = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        nf = 1
        while nf < 2 * n - 1:
            nf <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nf)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nf)[:2 * n - 1]

    m = float(conv.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _exact_score(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    n = a.size

    if n <= 8192:
        conv = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        nf = 1
        while nf < 2 * n - 1:
            nf <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nf)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nf)[:2 * n - 1]

    m = float(conv.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _try_state(state, a, exact=False):
    score_func = _exact_score if exact else _fast_score
    score = score_func(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return state

    if score < state[0] - 1e-12:
        arr = _clean_array(a)
        ss = float(arr.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            return state
        arr = arr / ss
        state[0] = float(score)
        state[1] = arr.copy()

        if score < _PRINT[0] - 0.0005 or time.monotonic() - _PRINT[1] > 20.0:
            print(f"[best] score={score:.8f} length={arr.size} t={time.monotonic():.1f}")
            _PRINT[0] = float(score)
            _PRINT[1] = float(time.monotonic())
    return state


def _convert_length(a, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = _clean_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]))
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()

    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return b / s


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)


def _make_expseq(n, lam):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    y = float(lam) * x
    y = y - float(y.max())
    f = np.exp(y)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return f / s


def _make_polyexp(n, coeffs):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = 2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False) - 1.0
    s = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    pw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for c in np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).ravel():
        if not np.isfinite(c):
            c = 0.0
        pw = pw * x
        s += float(c) * pw
    s = np.clip(s, -50.0, 50.0)
    f = np.exp(s)
    ss = float(f.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return f / ss


def _make_harmonic(n, coeffs):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    kmax = c.size // 2
    for k in range(1, kmax + 1):
        a = float(c[2 * k - 2])
        b = float(c[2 * k - 1])
        if not np.isfinite(a):
            a = 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(b):
            b = 0.0
        f += a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x) + b * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    ss = float(f.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return f / ss


def _make_blockseq(n, masses):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = _clean_array(masses)
    if m.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    m = np.maximum(m, 1e-12)
    ss = float(m.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-12:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    m = m / ss

    b = m.size
    base = n // b
    rem = n % b
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = 0
    for i in range(b):
        ln = base + (1 if i < rem else 0)
        if ln <= 0:
            continue
        val = float(m[i]) / float(ln)
        out[pos:pos + ln] = val
        pos += ln
    st = float(out.sum())
    if st <= 0.0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return out / st


def _make_rawseq(n, x):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = _clean_array(x)
    if a.size != n:
        a = _convert_length(a, n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    return a / s


def _de_objective(x):
    n = int(_DE_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return 100.0
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)

    if _DE_MODE == 1:
        a = _make_blockseq(n, x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 2:
        a = _make_polyexp(n, x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 3:
        a = _make_harmonic(n, x)
    elif _DE_MODE == 4:
        a = _make_rawseq(n, x)
    else:
        return 100.0

    s = _fast_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return 100.0
    return float(s)


def _softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / max(1, z.size))
    return e / s


def _smooth_sequence(p, width):
    width = int(width)
    if width <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    kernel = np.ones(width, dtype=np.float64) / float(width)
    q = np.convolve(p, kernel, mode="same")
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    return q / s


def _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, amp):
    n = z.size
    if n <= 2:
        z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n)
        z = z - z.mean()
        return np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    nfreq = max(1, min(n // 4, 12))
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, nfreq + 1):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp_k = float(amp) / float(k + 1)
        noise += amp_k * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
    z = z + noise
    z = z - z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)


def _small_local_optimize(seed, deadline, state, rng):
    a = _clean_array(seed)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size < 3 or a.size > 200 or s <= 1e-12:
        return state
    p = a / s
    n = p.size
    local_best = min(state[0] if np.isfinite(state[0]) else np.inf, _fast_score(p))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = p.copy()
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))

        if mode == 0:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i != j:
                eps = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.25))
                mv = float(p[i]) * eps
                q[i] -= mv
                q[j] += mv
        elif mode == 1:
            w = int(max(2, n // 8))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            en = min(n, st + w)
            q[st:en] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        elif mode == 2:
            q = p * (1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=n))
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            q[i] *= float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.0))

        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ss = float(q.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-12:
            continue
        q = q / ss
        sc = _fast_score(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best - 1e-10:
            p = q
            local_best = float(sc)
            _try_state(state, p)

    return state


def _smooth_adam_optimize(seed, n, deadline, state, rng):
    p = _convert_length(seed, int(n))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return state
    p = p / s
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 4:
        return state

    z = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    z = z - z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)

    idx = np.arange(n)
    sums = idx[:, None] + idx[None, :]

    mvec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vvec = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    betas = (0.90, 0.999)
    step = 0.10
    no_improve = 0
    local_best = min(state[0] if np.isfinite(state[0]) else np.inf, _fast_score(p))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        tau = min(40.0, 4.0 + 0.03 * float(t + 1))

        p = _softmax_z(z)
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        C = float(n) * c
        mC = float(C.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mC):
            break

        e = np.exp(tau * (C - mC))
        wsum = float(e.sum())
        if wsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            e = np.zeros_like(C)
            e[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
            wsum = 1.0
        w = e / wsum

        grad_p = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.sum(w[sums] * p[None, :], axis=1))
        grad_z = grad_p - p * float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
        scale = float(np.linalg.norm(grad_z))

        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-12:
            z = _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, 0.15)
            p = _softmax_z(z)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        grad_z = grad_z / scale
        t += 1
        b1, b2 = betas
        mvec = b1 * mvec + (1.0 - b1) * grad_z
        vvec = b2 * vvec + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_z * grad_z)

        bc1 = 1.0 - b1 ** t
        bc2 = 1.0 - b2 ** t
        z = z - step * (mvec / bc1) / (np.sqrt(vvec / bc2) + 1e-8)
        z = z - z.mean()
        z = np.clip(z, -35.0, 35.0)

        if (t % 3) == 0:
            p = _softmax_z(z)
            sc = _fast_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < state[0] - 1e-12:
                    state[0] = float(sc)
                    state[1] = p.copy()
                    if sc < _PRINT[0] - 0.0005 or time.monotonic() - _PRINT[1] > 20.0:
                        print(f"[adam] score={sc:.8f} len={n} tau={tau:.2f}")
                        _PRINT[0] = float(sc)
                        _PRINT[1] = float(time.monotonic())

                if sc < local_best - 1e-9:
                    local_best = float(sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    if step < 0.45:
                        step *= 1.04
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    if no_improve > 40:
                        no_improve = 0
                        step *= 0.82
                        if step < 0.02:
                            z = _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, 0.18)
                            p = _softmax_z(z)

    _try_state(state, p)
    return state


def _active_optimize(seed, n, deadline, state, rng):
    p = _convert_length(seed, int(n))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return state
    p = p / s
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 4:
        return state

    z = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    z = z - z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)

    step = 0.12
    no_improve = 0
    local_best = min(state[0] if np.isfinite(state[0]) else np.inf, _fast_score(p))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        C = float(n) * c
        mC = float(C.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mC):
            break

        tol = max(1e-10, 0.02 * mC)
        active = np.where(C >= mC - tol)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
        elif active.size > 120:
            active = np.argpartition(C, -120)[-120:]

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in active:
            k = int(kk)
            lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            i = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
            j = k - i
            grad[lo:hi + 1] += p[j]

        grad *= (2.0 * float(n) / float(active.size))
        grad_z = grad - p * float(np.dot(p, grad))
        scale = float(np.linalg.norm(grad_z))

        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-12:
            z = _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, 0.20)
            p = _softmax_z(z)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 40:
                no_improve = 0
                step = 0.20
            continue

        z_t = z - step * (grad_z / scale)
        z_t = z_t - z_t.mean()
        z_t = np.clip(z_t, -35.0, 35.0)
        p_t = _softmax_z(z_t)
        sc_t = _fast_score(p_t)

        if np.isfinite(sc_t) and sc_t < local_best - 1e-11:
            z = z_t
            p = p_t
            local_best = float(sc_t)
            _try_state(state, p)
            if step < 1.2:
                step *= 1.07
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            step *= 0.83
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 24:
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = False
                for w in (3, 5, 9, 15, 25):
                    ps = _smooth_sequence(p, w)
                    scs = _fast_score(ps)
                    if np.isfinite(scs) and scs < local_best - 1e-10:
                        p = ps
                        local_best = float(scs)
                        z = np.log(p + 1e-300)
                        z = z - z.mean()
                        z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
                        _try_state(state, p)
                        accepted = True
                        break
                if not accepted:
                    z = _add_smooth_noise(z, rng, 0.25)
                    p = _softmax_z(z)
                    step = 0.20

    _try_state(state, p)
    return state


def _random_seeds(t_end, state, rng, n_main):
    gh = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if gh is not None:
        arr = _clean_array(gh)
        if arr.size > 0:
            if arr.size > 20000:
                arr = _convert_length(arr, 4096)
            _try_state(state, arr)
            for nn in (256, 1024, 2048):
                _try_state(state, _convert_length(arr, nn))

    for nn in (32, 128, 512, 1024):
        _try_state(state, _make_uniform(nn))

    for lam in np.linspace(-3.5, 3.5, 19):
        _try_state(state, _make_expseq(n_main, float(lam)))

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        d = int(rng.integers(3, 7))
        c = rng.normal(0.0, 1.35, size=d)
        _try_state(state, _make_polyexp(n_main, c))

        K = 3
        h = rng.normal(0.0, 0.42, size=2 * K)
        _try_state(state, _make_harmonic(n_main, h))

        for b in (5, 8, 12, 18, 26):
            m = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(b, dtype=np.float64))
            _try_state(state, _make_blockseq(n_main, m))

        x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=n_main)
        q = np.exp(x - x.max())
        _try_state(state, q)

        q = _make_expseq(n_main, -0.8) * (1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_main, endpoint=False)))
        _try_state(state, q)

    return state


def _run_small_de(t_end, state, rng):
    if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
        return state

    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        for n in (6, 8, 10, 12, 16):
            for _ in range(3000):
                x = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
                _try_state(state, x)
        return state

    global _DE_MODE, _DE_N

    for n in (6, 8, 10, 12, 16, 24, 32):
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
            break
        try:
            _DE_MODE = 4
            _DE_N = int(n)
            bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
            pop = max(8, 8 + int(n // 2))
            res = _SO.differential_evolution(
                _de_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=90,
                popsize=float(max(2, min(4, 48 // n + 2))),
                tol=1e-7,
                seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                polish=False
            )
            a = _make_rawseq(n, res.x)
            _try_state(state, a)
            _try_state(state, _convert_length(a, 256))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("small DE failed:", type(exc).__name__, exc)

    return state


def _run_param_de(t_end, state, rng, n_main):
    if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
        return state

    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        while time.monotonic() < t_end:
            d = int(rng.integers(4, 8))
            _try_state(state, _make_polyexp(256, rng.normal(0.0, 1.8, size=d)))
            _try_state(state, _make_polyexp(n_main, rng.normal(0.0, 1.8, size=d)))
            m = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64))
            _try_state(state, _make_blockseq(256, m))
            _try_state(state, _make_blockseq(n_main, m))
        return state

    global _DE_MODE, _DE_N

    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 15.0:
        try:
            _DE_MODE = 1
            _DE_N = 256
            bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * 16
            res = _SO.differential_evolution(
                _de_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=160,
                popsize=3.0,
                tol=1e-7,
                seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                polish=False
            )
            _try_state(state, _make_blockseq(256, res.x))
            _try_state(state, _make_blockseq(n_main, res.x))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("block DE failed:", type(exc).__name__, exc)

    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 15.0:
        try:
            _DE_MODE = 2
            _DE_N = 256
            bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * 6
            res = _SO.differential_evolution(
                _de_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=160,
                popsize=3.0,
                tol=1e-7,
                seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                polish=False
            )
            _try_state(state, _make_polyexp(256, res.x))
            _try_state(state, _make_polyexp(n_main, res.x))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("polyexp DE failed:", type(exc).__name__, exc)

    if time.monotonic() < t_end - 8.0:
        try:
            _DE_MODE = 3
            _DE_N = 256
            bounds = [(-0.75, 0.75)] * 8
            res = _SO.differential_evolution(
                _de_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=140,
                popsize=3.0,
                tol=1e-7,
                seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                polish=False
            )
            _try_state(state, _make_harmonic(256, res.x))
            _try_state(state, _make_harmonic(n_main, res.x))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("harmonic DE failed:", type(exc).__name__, exc)

    return state


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)

    state = [np.inf, None]
    N_MAIN = 1024
    N_SMOOTH = 256

    _random_seeds(min(deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 30.0), state, rng, N_MAIN)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        _run_small_de(min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0), state, rng)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        _run_param_de(min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0), state, rng, N_MAIN)

    starts = []
    if state[1] is not None:
        starts.append(_convert_length(state[1], N_SMOOTH))
    starts.append(_make_expseq(N_SMOOTH, -1.0))
    starts.append(_make_expseq(N_SMOOTH, -0.45))
    starts.append(_make_polyexp(N_SMOOTH, rng.normal(0.0, 1.4, size=6)))
    starts.append(_make_harmonic(N_SMOOTH, rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=6)))
    starts.append(_make_blockseq(N_SMOOTH, rng.dirichlet(np.ones(12, dtype=np.float64))))

    scored = []
    seen = set()
    for st in starts:
        sc = _fast_score(st)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            kk = tuple(np.round(st, 6))
            if kk not in seen:
                seen.add(kk)
                scored.append((sc, st))

    scored.sort(key=lambda zz: zz[0])
    chosen = [zz[1] for zz in scored[:3]]
    if not chosen:
        chosen = [starts[0]]

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    per = max(2.0, min(240.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(chosen)))))
    for st in chosen:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
            break
        sub_end = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + per)
        _smooth_adam_optimize(st, N_SMOOTH, sub_end, state, rng)

    for n_opt in (512, 1024, 2048):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 70.0:
            break
        sub_end = min(deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + min(300.0, remaining * 0.35))
        seed_seq = state[1] if state[1] is not None else _make_expseq(n_opt, -1.0)
        _active_optimize(_convert_length(seed_seq, n_opt), n_opt, sub_end, state, rng)

    if state[1] is not None and state[1].size <= 96 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        _small_local_optimize(state[1], min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0), state, rng)

    if state[1] is not None:
        base = state[1]
        tried = set()
        for nn in (base.size, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
            nn = int(nn)
            if nn in tried:
                continue
            tried.add(nn)
            a = _convert_length(base, nn)
            _try_state(state, a, exact=True)

    if state[0] > 1.5027 and state[1] is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        final_n = int(state[1].size) if state[1].size >= 512 else 2048
        final_n = min(4096, max(512, final_n))
        sub_end = deadline - 10.0
        _active_optimize(_convert_length(state[1], final_n), final_n, sub_end, state, rng)

    if state[1] is None:
        state[1] = _make_expseq(1024, -1.0)
        state[0] = _fast_score(state[1])

    final_arr = _clean_array(state[1])
    ss = float(final_arr.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-12 or final_arr.size == 0:
        final_arr = _make_uniform(1024)
    else:
        final_arr = final_arr / ss

    final_list = [float(v) for v in final_arr]
    return final_list
```
